# Measured OI-change cadence

Generated by `python3 -m engine.oi_cadence` from recorded tick data (config v24). Market hours 09:15–15:40 IST (F-NSE-1). Interval = seconds between consecutive OI changes by exchange timestamp; intervals overlapping a recorded gap are excluded. p90 = nearest rank. COMBINED:<underlying> rows (token `combined`) use the union of that underlying's futures' change times (same-second changes count once).
